Djocjocarta den 22 Maart 1808.
23 May – 1808 15 Jun 1808 Yogya/Solo 1808 Dj. Br. 23.

## 29 May 1808
D asks for information on state of Yogya Court. Eng. replies that sudden arrival of D in Semarang on 12 May and raising of troops had caused HB II concern and invitation to send courtiers to Semarang not in old adat. But after receiving letter from Daendels 16 May, changes attitude and agrees to send delegation to Semarang and agrees that son of W should attend review of troops. [^p109s-1]

## 2 Jun 1808
19 cavalry horses to be sent from Yogya to Semarang. 6 xeroxes of list of HB II's troops, commanded by EO, paraded at Rajawinangun on 1 Jun.

## 4 Jun 1808
Because of sickness of R.T. Pringgakusuma, old P. Adiwijaya sent instead.

## 2 Jun 1808: Report on HB II's troop manoeuvres at Rajawinangun
1 Jun, general mustering of troops at Rajawinangun. The Crown Prince in N.E. part of kraton, accompanied out to Rajawinangun and 9 young nobles Panyio, [^p109s-2] noted in yellow camool and trousers, over green velvet, ovalled without wifs, afgezet and gold passement and black velvet mutsjes, all sitting on lounged Schimmel horses. Party post themselves at W. Bastion. Kraton after passing Paseban to see troops file out, go to J.E. of kraton, see HB II sitting and all Ratu/R. Ayu. Old Dolwerk (bastion) watching troops marching out. Greeted in a friendly fashion by HB II. Ride around kraton and out to Rajawinangun.

---

Sit in *Pagelaran* at Rajah Wihangun. Princes come to pay their respects. Midday meal — [Daendels] gives orders for troops to assemble in Regiments. 2 hours of exercising. Returns to Yogya at 4.00.

[Daendels]: *"Om zijn goede hoedanigheden, gevoegd bij een deftig en vriendelijk voorkomen van groot en klein geëerd word"* [^p110s-1] shows himself to be a true friend of the Dutch. Drinks a cup of tea and puts visitors to ask for it to be mixed with milk.

[Marginal Note: **USING MALAY**]
[Daendels] cries out at the top of his voice. To this follows to speak nothing else but Malay because that was the language which the Sultan and friends of the Dutch held with their people. [Marginal Note: **Use of Malay**]

[Marginal Note: *[Servations to D, 28 Jun. 1808 concerning PWD see Below]*]
5,050 troops take part in exercises = 2,337 muskets, but most not skilled in the use of them.

W.N. Servations to Daendels, 5 Jul. 1808 (Note) || Col. Gordon sent on commission by D to Dondr. to get examples of cloth patterns from Solo. 

[Marginal Note: *[#] eg. Nav. Weaving for uniforms of D's troops*]
[Marginal Note: **WEAVING for D's TROOP**]
T. Winter points out skilled weaver in Kerang Bolong. A weaver and simple weaving loom to have for dimming in a few days. [Marginal Note: **Weaving for D's Troops**]

N.W. Winter to Servations. 2 Jul. 1808 (Tangang)
Dimang of Kerang Bolong cannot read. Examining blue linen = no weaver in area able to make it. (But enough linen weavers in Tangang area). [Marginal Note: **Tangang area**]

Nov.-D. 5 July 1808
Letter of D of 28 Jun. To pain delivered at court.

Eng. to Nic. Veerkens, 11 Jul. 1808
Id. to D.
Lt. Detelle trying to get 38 Pak runners for cavalry in Yogya. But V. difficult to assemble.

15 Jul. 1808
Ningadirana/Totruna = 2 of HB II's subjects guilty of arson arrested.

---

15 Jun. – 15 Jul. Yogya / Solo 1808

*   **Attack on Bandar at Salatiga**
    *   15 Jun. 1808: Attack on Chinese Bandar at Salatiga = none of the Yogya were involved.
    *   Dated Semarang: Cavalry from Semarang round up band.
    *   Ninggaminggala - robber leader [transcribed as "Robba leada"] from Yogya still not apprehended.
    *   Ready Namawara.
    *   **Kècu [thieves/robbers; transcribed as "Kecus"] in Yogya.**
    *   20 ordinary kilu party = well armed - a musket captured worth 200 Rijkodaaldas + Japanese Broadswords (Samurai). [^p111s-1]
    *   MB II / PB IV had advised Daendels not to bring a large escort on journey to Nela - Surabaya. Only bring 200 Eur. cavalry / Artillery / Infantrymen.
    *   After return from Surabaya - D has idea of passing through Solo and making a visit incognito without ceremonial to Yogya. (MB II to be informed).

DR II – Aug 15 Jul. 1808
*   Commission under R.T. Wiryanegara [transcribed as "Wiryangara"] to be sent out to investigate attack on Bandar at Salatiga and to apprehend Ninggaminggala (letter Pieter Engelhard to Danureja II, 15 June 1808).

ID – ID. 15 Jun. 1808
*   DR II to send messengers to Kidnu under R.T. Wiryanegara to capture kècu [robbers].
    *   200 Sp. D. on head of robber leader [transcribed as "Robba leada"].
    *   50 of membug Robber Band.
*   Because D has idea of visiting Yogya on return journey, road between Solo-Yogya to be put into order. (Bridges).

---

"Daendels. Regulation on Ceremonial and Etiquette 28 Jul. 1808 van der Chijs Plakaat-boek."

62. 1808. H. W. DAENDELS.

aanmerking genomen hebbende, dat op Semarang bijna alle objecten, zo van levensmiddelen, als andere behoeften, in contante munt worden betaald en maar in enkele gevallen papieren geld wordt aangenomen; overwegende, dat op dit fundament bij besluit van den 17en Junij laatstleden een gunstige dispositie genomen is ten aanzien van de uitbetaling der tractementen aan de militairen aldaar, en considererende, dat zijne secretarissen en de verdere geëmploijeerden op hun bureau in 't zelfde geval verseren: besluit, dat, gerekend van den 1en dezer, de tractementen der secretarissen-generaal en van de verdere geëmploijeerden op derzelver bureau uitbetaald zullen worden, de helft in zilver geld en de weder helft in papieren van credit.

Het tractement der secretarissen-generaal bedroeg 1000 rijksdaalders 's maands: dat van een eersten kommies 250, dat van drie onder-kooplieden en gezworen klerken 150, dat van twee andere dito's 75, dat van twee ordinaire klerken 50 en dat van twee andere dito's 40 rd's 's maands.

Op den 7en December 1808 bepaalde Daendels nader, dat deze regeling zoude gelden, "waar en verwaards dezelve [secretarissen, enz.] zich ook in het gevolg en in dienst van Z. E. den Maarschalk en Gouverneur Generaal zullen bevinden". Zie ook 8 September 1808.

27 Julij. Opheffing der betrekking van tweeden regent van Demak.

Een der motieven voor deze opheffing was, "dat het aanstellen en onderhouden van één regent den gemeenen man zal soulageren en bijdragen om hem eenigzins tot verhaal te doen komen van de kwellingen en vexatiën, waaronder hij nu reeds zoo lange heeft gezucht". In ditzelfde besluit beweert Daendels, dat Demak "onder 't vorige bestuur van den gewezen Javasch gouverneur, Engelhard, zoo slegt is geadministreerd".

---

1808. H. W. DAENDELS. 63. [Stempel: ARSIP NASIONAL REPUBLIK INDONESIA]

Volgens zijn werk, Staat der Ned. O. I. bezittingen, bladz. 47, bedroegen de jaarlijkse uitgaven van de minsten regent ± 30.000 rijksdaalders.

28 Julij. Aanstelling van vier Javanen en twee Maleijers als klerken bij den gezworen translateur voor de Javaansche taal te Semarang.

Zij kregen een tractement van 16 rijksdaalders 's maands, en de Javanen den titel van mantri.

Vroeger waren zij "uit privé beurs" van den translateur betaald, maar dit kon niet langer, omdat de translateur een vast tractement had gekregen.

28 Julij. Reglement voor het ceremonieel, in acht te nemen door de residenten bij de hoven te Surakarta en te Yogyakarta.

Art. 1. De residenten aan de hoven, thans ministers van Zijne Majesteit den Koning van Holland zijnde en den persoon van den Maarschalk en Gouverneur Generaal aldaar representerende, zullen voortaan aan het hof moeten verschijnen in henne costume, dewelke zal bestaan in eene blauwen ge-kleeden rok, geborduurd met een olijftak en olijven in het goud langs den kraag, voorpanden, opslagen op de zakken en achterpanden, met ronde platte vergulde knopen, witte casimire broek met geborduurde kniebanden en wit camisool, witte zijde kousen en zwarte driekante hoed, met zwarte lis en cocarde.

Art. 2. Zij zullen aan de hoven gebruiken een groote paijong, welke van boven half aan de buitenrand verguld, in het midden ligt blaauw geschilderd en aan de binnen zijde geheel verguld moet zijn, welke paijong, verbeeldende het wapen van den Koning, hun bij ceremonieele gelegenheden overal nagedragen en inzonderheid bij verschijning aan de hoven zal moeten worden gebruikt.

Art. 3. Bij publieke audientien en verdere ceremonieele.

64 1808. H. W. DAENDELS.

Gelegenheden zullen zij met gedekte hoofden tot voor de vorsten naderen, welke van hunne zetels oprijzen, wanneer het opperhoofd 3 à 4 passen van hun af is, en, na hunne hoogheden gegroet te hebben, zich wederom zullen dekken en den vorst aanspreken, hetzij staande, hetzij zittende, zoo als de vorst door te blijven staan of zelve te gaan zitten haar zulks zal voorgaan, kunnende zij gemakshalve en des verkiezen de gedurende het discours ontdekken.

Art. 4. Bij audientie in den craton, zoo wel als op den troon of andere publieke gelegenheden, zullen zij hunne zitting op eene rei naast de vorsten, aan hunne linker zijde nemen, even als voor hun de gouverneurs van Java plegen te doen.

Art. 5. Zij zullen bij gelegenheid, dat zij de vorsten bij de hand leiden, zulks met gedekte hoofden doen, onder het gebruik der hun toegeschikte pajong, en alleen voor het nemen van zitplaats den vorst met ontdekten hoofde groeten en zich als voren wederom dekken.

Art. 6. Zij zullen de vorsten personeel geen wijn of sirie mogen presenteren, doch zal het eerste door een persoon, hetzij hofmeester of ander bediende, behoorlijk gekleed, moeten gedaan worden, die de vorsten en de residenten, den een na den anderen, den wijn zal aanbieden, ten zij de vorsten van deze ceremonie geheel mogen afzien, terwijl aan hunne hoogheden word overgelaten om zich de sirie door een der prinsen van den bloede te doen aanbieden.

Art. 7. Zij zullen de vorsten, bij gelegenheid, dat dezelve hun een bezoek geven, beneden aan de trap recipieren en na binnen begeleiden, doch zal dit aan den kroonprins en verdere prinsen van den bloede niet geschieden en dezelve in de zaal gerecipieerd worden, terwijl de rijksbestuurder en de regenten tot hun zullen moeten naderen om hunne complimenten af te leggen.

Art. 8. Zij, nog hun gevolg, zullen bij ontmoeting van den vorst of eenige naaststaande op publieke wegen of andersints, in hun rijtuig zijnde, te paard zittende of te voet gaande, niet vermogen stil te houden of uit den weg te gaan. Doch zal de wellevendheid in zoodanig een geval moeten in acht genomen en den vorst met het halve lijf buiten het rijtuig en de andere grooten mede met beleefdheid moeten worden gegroet.

Art. 9. Zij zullen zich altijd, hetzij naar de authentie of andere gelegenheden in de craton of elders gaande, van een rijtuig, hetzij met 2 of 4 paarden bespannen, moeten bedienen en zich als dan van een detachement van een wachtmeester en 12 dragonders te paard moeten laten vergezellen, terwijl buiten dien de grenadiers lijfwacht, als gewoonlijk, op haar post in de craton zal moeten wezen.

Art. 10. Zij zullen voortaan, bij vergezelling van eenen officieelen brief, denzelven vooral in eene koets moeten laten voortdragen en denzelven onmiddellijk in een andere koets volgen onder het gewoon ceremonieel, zonder denzelven als bevoren te voet te aan den craton te verzellen.

Art. 11. Zij zullen hun karakter bij alle gelegenheden soutineren en niets doen, hetgeen de waardigheid van hunne representatie zoude krenken, behandelende de vorsten steeds met alle behoorlijk respect.

Dit reglement is vastgesteld onder nadere goedkeuring van de betrokken, inlandsche vorsten. De Soesoehoenan heeft zich daartegen niet verzet, maar de Sultan van Yogyakarta wel. Zie Daendels, staat der Ned. O. I. bezittingen, bladz. 94.

29 Julij. Aanmaak van papieren geld voor Banda.

Is goedgevonden en verslaan tot een proeve voor Banda alhier te laten aanmaken rds 20000 aan papieren van credit, verdeeld ter waarde van:

1000 ps à rds 5 tot ........................ rds 5000,
500 » » 10 » ........................ » 5000,
400 » » 15 » ........................ » 6000 en
200 » » 20 » ........................ » 6000,

om, evenals de bij secreet besluit dezer regeering van den 5 [^p115s-1]

---

22 Jul. – 30 Jul. 1808
Engelhard to Daendels, 1808, Yogya / Sala

22 Jul. 1808
Letter of D. 16/17 July for HB II arrives. Although HB II does not speak about appointment of Prince of Madura to Sultan Sepuh, [he] feels somewhat humiliated by appointment. Daendels assures HB II that treating both Sala / Yogya in objective fashion and gives necessary orders to R. Rongga to allow filling of teak timber in E. Mancanagara. Eng. to try to bring back Yogya court to duty as a vassal of King of Holland.

Eng. to HB II
22 Jul. 1808 (24 Jumadilawal 1735)
D. does not send any troops of HB II or PB IV. English annoyance at attacks of robbers from Yogya on government lands, e.g., recent attack near Jambu. Considers it due to negligent policy premeditated by district regents.

20 July
PB IV to gather troops for inspection by D on journey to Sala.
PWD had not been given permission to go to Klaten. Only help D asks of HB II is to be allowed to cut wood from E. M. provinces for fortifications in E. Mancanagara. For pillars in Fort Lodewijk in Nea.
Yogya would have to accept new ceremonial which D after return to Semarang on 28 Jul. 1808 would draw up & send to court.
D would not come incognito to Yogya but would await another suitable occasion to visit + full ceremonial.

Suratings to Daendels 22 Jul. 1808
Ceremonial for D's visit to Sala.

Eng - Veekens, 25 Jul. 1808
5 chests of opium arrive in Yogya from Semarang. Sells at 20 Sp.D. per catie in Padang. Tan Jin Sing only person who could be trusted + chests on credit = wants to sell chests at Sp.D. 2,300 although in Kempong Cina in Semarang can be bought for Sp.D. 1,800.

---

Surat-ins: van Braam, 25 Jul. 1808. Troops from Solo to Tegal via Semarang, 500 men to help put down Cirebon disturbances. A v. active Solo prince.

Eng. to Veekens, 27 Jul. 1808. MB II [1] informed of Cirebon rebels = Kyai Kulur/Bagus Rangin/Durrahman. MB II amazed that Dutch had not been able to capture them. Give necessary orders in case rebels cross into Yogya territory. + Menjangan to be sent to Banyumas. To seek out hiding places [of] rebels.

27 Jul. 1808. Pg Bandar at Panawangan in Grobogan. E. MN [2] provinces rented out to Rongga + Bandar at Panawangan by R.T. Natrakusuma. + NK to Chinese Babah Hoekong. Only help D. [3] asks of MB II is to leave all wood in E. MN for six months for fortifications in E. Java. To negotiate this separately + Rongga from E. MN.

Eng. to Daendels, 19 Jul. 1808. 11 April. N. Engelhard - Gov. of Java's N.E. coast. Orders making of following items in Yogya: 198 kasketten [4], 198 rijstangen met knopjes, 198 pr. sporen, 190 pr. roskammen, 190 borstels, 190 kommando koutros [5].

2e Res. to J. Groshoff van Hortmann. Goes to Semarang to deliver 60 black saddles ordered by D. + britzen/laarzen [boots].

Of list dated Yogya 25 Jul. 1808.

Eng. to Daendels, 30 Jul. 1808. Letter from R.T. Pringgakusuma brought by 2 Ng. [6] from Rawa stating that pirates had landed in Negara Weddi. + Pringgakusuma to move to spot + 80 men armed w. muskets + pikes. Eng. lends artillery from Yogya garrison.***

### Marginalia

*   **Left Margin (red):** "Cirebon Troubles"
*   **Left Margin (red):** "LEATHER GOODS. ORDERED IN YOGYA"
*   **Right Margin (red):** "wood from E. MN"
*   **Right Margin (red):** "Leather goods ordered in Yogya"
*   **Right Margin (red):** "whip/boots"
30 Jne. — 9 Aug. 1808
Yogya Solo
1808 P. Br. 23.

30 Jne. 1808: Servations to Daendels, Reply to PB IV sent to Daendels [illegible] 13 July.

25 Jul. 1808: Eng to Daendels, 25 corges of Javanese cloth sent to Yogya from Semarang. For turning into montering uniforms. For D's troops. @ total cost of f 1,004.

2 Aug. 1808: ID to ID. (28 Jul. 1808) Letter of D including new order on ceremonial etiquette. Sent to HB II. [Note: *Borduurse* for new costume of ministers. See ceremonial p. 63.]

* In Yogya Desa Kiankong in Bagelin. *Kraman from Cirebon.* A kraman arrives. Small in height & moustache / bakke [?] from Cirebon. 40 years old. Dressed in a great gingang kabay. Nobody dares touch him/arrest him because considered holy.

3 Aug. 1808: ID to ID. HB II had held a meeting of Nayakas to discuss new ceremonial. 

> "Dit stuk nog niet afgedaan is, echter over vrij veel ongerustheid heerscht" [This piece has not yet been finished, as there prevails quite a lot of unrest] as well as in gemosd of various, courties, [?] + HB II preparing to remonstrate about various clauses.

Those in favour of accepting ceremonial: 
- P. Ngabehi Danureja II
- Inner Regent R.T. Purwadipura
- Jager Reg. R.T. Mangnadipura
- Outu [?] Kyai J. Windanigara

But during presence of PWDN troops in Yogya = Recently re-appointed Head Inner Regent R.T. Sumadiningrat [Note: *V. ceremonial NP Reapptd.*] counsels HB II not to accept this ceremonial because would be humiliating for HB II.

Eng. tries to persuade HB II that this was not the case; ceremonial necessary outcome of changed constitution in Holland. R. Rongga / R.T. Natadiningrat do not express an opinion during meeting.
But dejected attitude of Rongga and tearful face (also fox—Rongga) if not a trick of this "shy fox" seems to indicate that Rongga prepared to consent to acceptance of memorial but highly embarrassed about it.

#### SP/Rongga

**Nervatins -> D, 4 Aug. 1808**
Gives new ceremonial to court of Solo. Patih Danuningrat arrests [the] Robbus in Blora to be sent to Semarang.

**Eng -> D, 7 Aug. 1810**
Reply of HB II to D’s letter and project ceremonial sent to Daendels. Revisions [regarding] difficulties and 3 articles of ceremonial. But agrees to accept project ceremonial. Would like to see 3 arts. changed. Eng. advises change to keep peace and HB II.

**Eng -> D, 6 Aug. 1810**
Letter from Bp. Grobogan R.T. Sasrakusuma about bandar of Panawangan which government wants to take over. [To] R. Rongga, 4 Aug. 1808 (11 Jumadilakhir 1735): Dismissal of Babah Hockong as bandar of Panawangan. But considers it undue interference in his admin. Wants to see Babah Hockong re-appointed. Babah Hockong had already paid wachtgeld of 1,500 N.D.

#### Mil. Hospital Yogya (kwaad pamper in fort.)

**Eng -> Daendels, 9 Aug. 1808**
Sickness amongst Amboinese in garrison—needs to [set] up [a] mil. hospital [in] Yogya. Purchases hospital space to buy house at [the] Nehalt by river for N.D. 1,500. To [be] in [a] fort by running river. Hospitaler desirous to be placed in charging patients.

**Eng -> Daendels, 9 Aug. 1808**
Birthday of King Louis Napoleon. Cannons to be fired. 45 gun salute and gardens to be lit up.
## 1808 Yogyakarta

**30 June – 9 August 1808**

*   **30 June 1808:** Negotiations to Daendels, reply by PB IV sent to Daendels.
*   **13 July:** Engagement to Daendels: 25 corges of Javanese cloth sent to Yogya from Semarang for turning into monteering uniforms for DJ troops. Total cost of f. 1,004.
*   **28 July 1808:** ID to ID: Letter of ID including new order on ceremonial etiquette.
*   **2 August 1808:** Sent to HB II: Bonhurge for new costume of ministers. [^p120s-1]
*   **In Yogya:** Desa Kriankong in Bagelin. A *kraman* [^p120s-2] from Cirebon arrives. Small in height, moustache, bakke, 40 years old, dressed in a great gingang kabaya. Nobody dares touch him or arrest him because he is considered holy.
*   **3 August 1808:** ID to ID: HB II had held a meeting of Nayakas to discuss new ceremonial.
    *   *“Dit stuk nog niet afgedaan is, en hier over vrij veel ongerustheid heerst”* [^p120s-3] as well as in gemosd of various courtiers, to test HB II preparing to remonstrate about various clauses.
    *   **Those in favour of accepting ceremonial:**
        - P. Ngabehi Danureja II
        - Inner Regent R.T. Purwadipura
        - Inner Reg. R.T. Mangundipura
        - Outu Kyai J. Nindatigana
    *   **But during presence of Dutch troops in Yogya:** Recently re-appointed Head Inner Regent R.T. Sumadiningrat [^p120s-4] counsels HB II not to accept this ceremonial because it would be humiliating for HB II.
    *   Eng. tries to persuade HB II that this was not the case. Ceremonial necessary outcome of changed constitution in Holland.
    *   R. Rongga / R.T. Natadiningrat: Do not express an opinion during meeting.

---

But dejected attitude of Rongga & tearful face (only Cox - Rongga) if not a trick of this "why Cox" seems to indicate that Rongga prepared to counsel acceptance of memorial but highly embarrassed about it.

#### SP/Rongga

**Suratins -> D, 4 Aug. 1808**
Gives new ceremonial to court of Solo. Patin Danuningrat arrested & robbed in Blora to be sent to Semarang.

**Eng -> D, 7 Aug. 1810**
Reply of HB II to D's letter & project ceremonial sent to Daendels. Raises difficulties & 3 articles of memorial. But agrees to accept project ceremonial. Would like to see 3 arts. changed.
*   *Note: Eng. advises change to keep peace & HB II*

**Eng -> D, 6 Aug. 1810**
Letter from Bp. Grobogan R.T. Sasrakusuma = about Bandar of Panawangan which Govmt. wants to take over.

**R.T. Sasrakusuma to R. Rongga, 4 Aug. 1808 (11 Jumadilakir 1735)**
Dismissal of Babah Hockong as Bandar of Panawangan. But considers it undue interference in his admin. Wants to see Babah Hockong re-appointed. Babah Hockong had already paid Lachtgeld of 1,500 N.p.D.

**Mil. Hospital Yogya**
(Kwaade dampem in Ront.)

**Eng -> Daendels, 9 Aug. 1808**
Sickness amongst Ambonese in garrison — needs to purchase hospital space -> to buy house Lt. Nehalt by Riven for N.p.D. 1,500. To N.g Fort by Running River. Hospitaler dusters to be placed in charge of patients.

**Eng -> Daendels, 9 Aug. 1808**
Birthday of King Louis Napoleon = Cannon to be fired. 45 gun salute & gardens to be lit up.

---

11 Aug. - 15 Aug. Yogya sala 1808 D.J. Br. 23

11 Aug. 1808: P.B. IV accepts & pleasure proposed visit of Panguang Madura & his brothers & accepts invitation for 25 Aug. for party to celebrate [?] accession of Louis Napoleon as King of Holland. (? Daendels also to be present). P.B. IV gives order to capture tigers at sala territory bordering on Semarang for party on 25 Aug.

Van B - D, 10 Aug. 1808: Arrives back from Semarang on 9 Aug. at 6:00 p.m. Patih comes to greet V.B. Favourably impressed by new ceremonial & invites V.B. to audiance [sic] & P.B. IV. Asks for 500 Solo Batun to be sent to Bat. Bovenlanden. Patih says would happen directly.

G. van Wortman to D, 12 Aug. 1808: Besl. v. G.G. 8 Aug. 1808 (see van der Chys, N.I. Plakaatboek 1602-1811, Vol. 15, p. 87). Title of Res. on N. Coast Residencies exchanged for that of Prefect. It is 4 oostensche gouvernementen & on Island of Madura under-prefects [^p122s-1].

Wortman asks for a raise for his "personele tegenwoordigen inkomsten".

Eng. to D, 14 Aug. 1808: Sick Ambonese soldiers in garrison to be cared for in house of Lt. Schalk. Lack of space in Yogya but open to cold nights & S.E. wind. Large mil. hospital in Sala & disposal min. house if necessary. Need for building bamboo logis for sick military men.

G.W. Netting, hospitalia in Yogya to P. Els, 13 Aug. 1808: Lack of mil. hospital in Port & No. [?] given in s' [?] Eur. kampong too open to northern winds.

---

Decision to set up bamboo lodgings for patients in neighbourhood of Fortress.

*   **Fear of PWP as by HB II** [^p123s-1]
*   **Eng. to D.** (14 Aug. 1808): Fear HB II that PWD as Col. Comm. Legion has evil intentions v. Yogya. HB II cancels exp. of P. Dipakusuma Mangkukusuma Mangkuningrat & wishes to retain Yogya troops in city to be able to fend off danger.
*   P. W. D. garrison duties in Klaten & wants undertaking that no Yogya troops to be ordered to march elsewhere.
*   Now everything peaceful again.

> D. DEMANDING D'S BANISHMENT FROM YOGYA.
> * D's demands that Natadiningrat should be banished should not be carried out because testimony against him based on that one man.
> * **Daendels [^p123s-2] nuks banishment of D.**
> * Eng. would keep watchful eye on ND — to come to supper in Res. that evening & Patil & other conditions.

*   **Eng. to D.** (14 Aug. 1808): After receiving letter D (12 Aug. 1808) answers Patil/HB II that PWD as Col. Comm. could take no step without orders of Government. Fear that PWD badly disposed towards Yogya. HB II cancels exp. of Dipakusuma.

*   **PLANNING D TO VISIT YOGYA**

*   **Eng. to D.** (15 Aug. 1808): Arrival letter D written from Bojong 13 Aug. D gives orders for 3,000 troops to march to Klaten/Yogya. Eng. summoned to court by HB II to explain why D no suspicions of Yogya.
*   HB II publicly states in presence of Nayakas that does not mistrust Daendels.
*   Tells HB II that following Wed. v. early Eng. & Patil to go to Klaten to wait on Daendels.
*   Nee. Groenhof to accompany HB II (? to Klaten).
*   Does not see need to proceed v. Natadiningrat yet.
*   Ideas locking P. Ngabei up in Fort for various misdeeds.

## 15 Aug. – 26 Aug. [1808]

**Yogya/Solo 1808 [D. Br. 23]**

#### MB II to Eng.
**Mon. 22 Jumadilakir 1735 (15 Aug. 1808)**
Danureja gives notice that [he] would come to Yogya on [a specific date] to arrange all outstanding affairs. MB II [is] to bring 3,000 men because MB II does not [feel] *vastigheid* [security] in *mijn sentimenten* [my sentiments]. [He] is to talk to MB II about everything in person. MB II [is] disappointed that [he is] cast in such a bad light to D.

[He] had planned to send troops under Dipokusumo/MK/MP to Bagelen/Kedu to keep an eye on PWD troops who were to be sent to Cilacap. [He] but cancels the expedition when answered by Eng. that [he] had nothing to fear from PWD as Col. had government orders.

Orders given for setting up temp. barracks and arranging food/vivres for D’s troops.
* MB II to await D at Dimangan.
* CP [to await D] at Papringan.
* MP/Mangunkusumo [to await D] at Kalasan.
* Danureja outer [to await D] at Kletin.
* Nayakas to accompany Eng.

#### Van Braam to D. (20 Aug. 1808)
Eng. proposes to come to Solo following Monday (22 Aug.) for a 1 day visit. [He] and Van B [are] not to go to Semarang until D gets back. (From E. Nawa).

* **Marginal note:** Madura connections & Solo
* **Marginal note:** News from Yogya = Seems that MB II takes things passively.

#### Eng. – P. (18 Aug. 1808)
Letter to D from Solo 18 Aug. 1808. Delivers letter and new ceremonial to MB II in kraton. Payung of Eng. carried by an old Nyai in kraton. Received by old gentleman in an unusually courtous & friendly fashion.

---

HB II receives letter well. No problem, raw memorial. Marching away of Dutch troops creates relief at what had taught NB a lesson.

**Eng - Daendels**
18 Aug. 1808: 300 Jav. Kludges sent to Semarang as on orders of D. for making necessary stukken for Mil.

ID. to ID.
22 Aug. 1808: PB IV agrees to meet Eng. at court on visit to Solo.

Van B -> D.
23 Aug. 1808: PB IV on occasions rest for Louis Nap. to marry off P. Panular [^p125s-1] and dig late P. Sumadiningrat wants to excuse... [^p125s-2]

*   Nending an invitation: Refuse it! Despite a hint, indication of growing animosity of PB IV - HB II according to Van B - PB IV less afraid of HB II than earlier.
*   Van B lets no opportunity to encourage feelings of animosity in PB IV.
*   Because absence of PWD from Solo, Van B has no information about Yogya. PWD enjoyed retailing bits of gossip about Yogya -> Solo Res. [^p125s-3]

Madurese Tumenggungs and P. Madura amusing themselves after court fashion - much liked by Solo princes. **Bad feelings.**

ID. to ID.
26 Aug. 1808: 4 Jagas severely wounded by Javanese. Dutch Jagas in connection with rest for L. Napoleon. [^p125s-4]

*   Jav. go to kampong near Rest to get a Ronggeng girl. Pay husband money, but after receiving it, door shut in their faces.
*   Jagas get 6/7 comrades to get back girl or money. But on entering kampong, party attacked and 14 badly wounded.
*   Jagas unharmed in kampong, armed with rifles.
*   Dutch Institutes investigations - says there were 30 jagas in kampong.

---

24 Aug. – 28 Aug. 1808 Yogyakarta [P.B. Br. 23]

26 Aug. 1808: PB IV + ratu [queen] + other warriors attend feast for Louis Napoleon from 5 p.m. yesterday to 3.15 a.m. Engelhard had still not arrived in Solo.

21 Aug. 1808: Having back [decided on] turning Government building, Mesterburgh, an hour from Yogya after [to be] military hospital in fort. Empty plot of land owned by Tan Jin Sing at Res. House surrounded by wall to be turned into a temp. hospital.

Leaving G. Wiese from Rimbang 15 Aug. [Pres. Houtbouncheng] Bp. Padhangan / Panolan / Madiun. All officials to present themselves in Yogya noon. Concerning [the] order concerning felling of timber [cutting wood in E. MN] to be informed about.

Complaints to Sultan that answers to [the] D’s [Daendels'] always belated. D's [message] to [the] *vader-in-law* [vertigden trainieren van z.h.n. antwoord] MB II, P. Mangkunegara, R.T. Purwadipura, and Taner Bp. and Bawat/Payong taken away from him. Because [they] had been slow in delivering request of Eng. to visit kraton; Col. Gerlach. R.T. Nindhitagara, R.T. Prawirawinata take over D’s [the Daendels'] trips.

29 Aug. 1808: Van Braam [to] D., 2 kampong dwellers (also prajurits of PB IV) who had wounded Jager. To be given military punishment. To walk gantleting 400 soldiers + rotting-plagen [caning] + afterward to be killed. Punishment to take place on following Thurs. in public on Paseban in presence PB IV. [Did D demand a more severe punishment?][^p126s-1]

Eng. [Daendels]: Torture of Stockdale = punishment of Nilib. of PB IV in Solo for rapidity by using upas poison. [Punishments abolished by Reggles 1812][^p126s-2]

---

20 Aug. – 21 Aug. 1808 Yogya/Solo 1808 Pj. Br. 23.

**26 Aug. 1808**
Van Braam -> D, P B IV + Retut otha wanties, attend feast for Louis Napoleon from 5 p.m. yesterday to 3.15 a.m. Engelland had still not arrived in Solo.

**21 Aug. 1808**
Having Back. Eng -> P, Ideag Turning Govnat Bldg. Kusterburgh. ½ an hour from Yogya after Bldg. mil. Hospital in Fort. Empty plot of land (owned by Jan Jin Sing) nr. Res. House, surrounded by a wall to be turned into a Temp. Hospital. [^p127s-1]

All officials to present themselves in Yogya noon concerning D'N ordas concerning telling of timber in E. MN. [^p127s-2]

Complaints to Sultan that answers to D'N always belated. D'N's [^p127s-3] veltijd van traineren van Z.H.H. aantwoord [^p127s-4].

HB II PIdMinnis R.T. Purwadipura and Janer Bp. & Bawat/payong taken away from him. Because had been slow in delivering request of Eng. to visit kraton Col. Gerlach. R.T. Nindhigara, R.T. Prawirawinata take over P.S. Trips.

**25 Aug. 1808**
Van Braam -> D, 2 kampong dwellers (also prajurit of P IV) who had wounded Jagen. [^p127s-5] To be given military punishment. To walk gauntlet 400 soldiers + Rotting-plagen [^p127s-6] = Caress [^p127s-7] + afterward to be killed. Punishment to take place on following Thurs. in public on Paseban in presence P IV. [^p127s-8]

Eng. Torture of Stockdale = punishment of Nilib. of P IV in Solo for impudity by using opas poison. [^p127s-9]

---

P.B. Madurat Brothers to leave following day.

P. Danur M. a/g P. Danuningrat on 27 Aug 1808 had not been informed. P.B. IV's troops sent to Negara Kidul (Negara Widhi) in connection with pirates but troops comm. not active. P.B. IV shown displeasure at having to provide 500 laborers from Banyumas for Bat. Daendels / necessary orders given by Dan. Jum. Sumanegara (Bp. Banyumas) at present in Nale to return to P.B.

In kraton: Prabayéksa. 3 houses of Ratu, all separate. Houses of R. Ayur / Niki / HB II = Gintang. + small pindhapa to J.J. Prabayéksa = Bangsal Ams.

Bangsal Rukma: Pindhapa, house which HB II usually sits when receives visitors in kraton.
1st gate: Driminsanti, 2 pindhapas open houses.
2nd gate: Mandhungan + " " in middle plain = Witana Mandhungan.

High ground: Lamadhuwur. 1 + pindhapa g Sultan Witana Lamadhuwur.
Lower level of " + pillar = Pagelaran.
Bangsal Pingawit: place for Sultan to sit.
Open house R/H = " Pingapit
Waringin kurung
Place for prisoners = Glad hag.

Rear gate of kraton = Gadhing.
W. gate of kraton = Gamping.

N.E. of kraton: Kadipatin.

## Prajurit arahan

Eng - D, 29 Aug. 1808: HB II besides troops already summoned = calls up troops from Desas.
Solo (Natawijaya): Ranga blamed to have summoned kirigan troops when he had gone to Semarang in June to visit D.
But Bp. Pedangan says: troops summoned only usually work force from MN provinces. At Mulud, none being prepared to accompany patih to go on journey to meet D at Semarang.

Lt. H. Vogel (Tangol ?GK), 20 Aug. 1808: Sols to Rewa 19 July + R.T. Pringgakusuma to investigate case of Robbas. (R.T. Mangundirana?)

R. Rongga PWD -> DRI, 22 Aug. 1808: Rongga had given orders to subjects in Madina to get arms / houses at ready. Had given these orders before going to Semarang in June, so would have sufficient escort on journey. Bring all kirigan from Maduan [armed] + allow them to go home after Mulud.
## Yogya 1808

**1808**

**P.J. Br. 23**

Eng. [Engelhard], 9 Aug. 1808. 
In fact 27 May 1808. In June = 250 Javanese from Mataram attack post of Bunder in Oonthoek [?]. [^p131s-1]
Flee into woods in direction of Kantanara (in Yogya territory). 

Groenhoff [^p131s-2] tells P. II [Pakubuwana II] on Eng's [Engelhard's] journey to Semarang (?) who sends out commission under a Mantri [^p131s-3] F. mn. [F. mon.] & Eur. corporal Pieter Gulin.
Report delivered on return of commission yesterday. But can find nothing.
Warns P. II not to allow subjects to make razzias over border into government territory & allow criminals to hide in VL. [^p131s-4]

Trust to get P. II to agree to holding a conference at Klaten [^p131s-5] & Res. [Resident] of Solo/Yogya. Eventually results in Sept. 1808 Treaty on criminals. 
Daendels [^p131s-6] had postponed conference until after Patih's had paid their respects to him in Semarang in July. 

Pieter Gulin -> P. Eng. & 2 Royal Mantris Angabaya, Nantawikrama, 19 Jul. 1808, to investigate affair in Kertasana & 2 Solo [^p131s-7] Mantris.
Read out to an audience of nearly 500 people in Bunder (many Chinese). But local inhabitants refuse to give information.
I Solo robba from Dintr. [Distrikt?] of Aniok shot dead.
Travel into forest for 2 days without inhabitants & many tigers. Big river runs through the middle.

Chinese of Bunder complain that Jav. [Javanese] give no help during difficulties.

R. Adip. [Raden Adipati] Danuningrat (Solo) to P. II.
Attack on Chinese in Bunder in Nurab Aya area (1 Rabingulakir 1735) & houses in Bunder Bhant. [^p131s-8]

---

Sending out a Solo mission to investigate plundering.
(See another letter of E-D, dated 11 Aug. 1808) [^p132s-1]

#### Ex-Daendels, 11 Aug. 1808
In *besluit* of G.G. P. Eng. needs him promotion to President of College of Sherpema [^p132s-2] in Batavia. Wants thanks Daendels for appointment. But Eng. to stay on as Res. in Yogya until Nov.
1. In order to have honour of receiving Daendels in Yogya.
2. To be present at paying of homage by rulers.
3. To work out agreement + Solo on *rodi* mood [^p132s-3] in Bovenlanden. (Klaten Agreements sept. 10 Oct. 1808)
4. To get income from bird's nests harvest in Antrom. [^p132s-4]
5. To defray enormous costs of his position as Opperhoofd [^p132s-5] during next 4 months. Income as Minister at Yogya not enough even for running an economical household; it cannot pay for daily bread.

#### J. van Braam
11 Aug. 1808
PB IV [^p132s-6] accepts all clauses of new regulation on ceremonial etiquette without demur. Makes gamelan play on *Lama Dhuwur* as sign of acceptance. Is somewhat PB IV v. friendly + pleased at D's expression of friendship in appointing Gusti Timun (P. H. B. Nong PB IV + Ratu Pamekasan) as Lt. of cavalry. [^p132s-7] Gives Brevet of appointment. At same time = causes PB IV great pleasure; tears start from eyes of Ratu.

PB IV seems pleased at new ceremonial in that it increases his prestige. If PB IV comes out of kraton, he is usually accompanied by women (old/young); in that case one kraton Nyai would carry *res. payong*. [^p132s-8]

Death of maître d'hôtel (*Hofmeester*) causes difficulties for Van Braam; another to be sent from Salatiga.

PB IV wants to know if Daendels still needs *prau ping impat* of Solo in Surabaya for another expedition (had taken D + Patih Danuningrat to Surabaya) if not, wants it returned to Solo + merchandise.

R.T. Natawijaya (Bp. Panolan) had not given orders to his followers to assemble arms/horses—only small workforce to Yogya.

Ibid. R.T. Nataregara.

Haji from Yogya.

## Raffles-Minto
### Daendels' Declaration to Patichs, 1808 Sept.

Raffles-Minto coll. vol. 18. Memoirs of Java at the time of the capture. R. 1148/18.

*Memo delivered by PB IV to W. Robinson, 24 Sept. 1811.*

#### 24 Sept? June/Sept? 1808.
**Daendels' declaration to Patichs at Semarang**

I receive with much pleasure and sincerity the homage of the Susuhunan [^p135s-1] through his Prime Minister and further ambassadors.

I do not consider this solemnity in the light of homage of a vassal to his lord paramount, the feudal system having been abolished in Europe, but I look upon it the same as congratulation on my safe arrival on this island and on the commencement of the administration of H.M. possessions in India.

The East India Company and the Republic of the United Provinces had lost their former influence in Europe, but the election of the Emperor's brother Louis Napoleon to the throne of Holland has caused the political influence of that country to be re-established both by adopting a more energetic mode of administration and by a most intimate union with the mightiest Empire in the world. It is the wish of King Louis to promote the happiness of his subjects on the Island of Java and to give them peace, prosperity and a benevolent government.

And I do solemnly declare in the name of His Majesty the friend and protector of the Princes and inhabitants of Java that I will...

---

endearour to maintain peace and to render the inland of Java as prosperous as possible.

Signed,

H.W. Gezelschap
(copy)
## 10 Oct. – 17 Oct. 1808 — Yogyakarta

**10 Oct. 1808**

Eng. [Engelhard] to D. [Daendels]: Thanks D. for hospitality during recent stay in Semarang. 10 Oct. 1808 HB II’s [Hamengkubuwana II] emissaries arrive. Back in Yogya, greeted with proper ceremony. Bring letter from Nic. Gen. Veekens [likely referring to Daendels’ subordinates/representatives] and compliments of D. to HB II. Tells HB II of reasons for D.’s hurried departure from Semarang to Batavia (not letting on to Eng.’s view of it) and trying 3rd/4th hand to sound out HB II’s [reaction] to Govmt’s [Government’s] financial situation. Already known by many Yogya courtiers: *poor financial situation of Government.* Raising of salary of minister. Van B. [Van Braam] to come to Yogya.

**17 Oct. 1808**

Van B. greeted by HB II op zijn manier [in his own way] in friendly fashion. Astonishing difference in friendliness in Yogya. HB II cannot speak Malay: difficult to converse with him. Has a norty [naughty/malicious] wild look in his eyes (verwilderd oog van het oog) which indicates suspicion and fear.

*Rampog macan* = Tendons of leg of karbonw [kerbau/buffalo] cut by tiger. Refuses to fight. 2nd tiger — jumps clean out of ring and killed behind place where Sultan was sitting. This incident, which had never occurred before during Rampog HB II, caused many to make conjectures with regard to me. HB II makes Van B. a compliment and says that occurred in V.B.’s honour. CP [Crown Prince] seems a really nice person, very affable, talkative, but like all those around HB II, deathly afraid of Sultan. Not allowed to see interior of kraton, but only estates/pendanggrahan which were beautifully laid out. 

Forms poor opinion of Rongga’s character: very dangerous person, very irascible, resentful and enterprising who gives in to nothing (die maar in niets toegeeft).

---

Rongga holds up *pran pinghupat* (Solo) on return from Surabaya and able to make his opinions prevail over those of Pati and HB II.

Solo court complains about 35 *pran pinghupat* held up by Rongga on Solo River. Rongga says he had released them [to a] commission to investigate. On return journey from Surabaya, all other *bandars* in consideration had let his flotilla pass by without paying toll, but only at Bandar of Rongga had they been held up. [He] cannot reply, but [is] nonetheless willing to release *prans*. BLADD. MS. 12303 J. 118v on Sa. Bl. Add Ms. 12303, Eng acts v. cordially in affair [and] HB II suggests to await their decision until result of commission.

On last day, Van B[raam] rides out of fort in carriage. On etiquette: to go to Residency [is] just opposite. Rongga cuts Van B's path and cuts dead by Rongga riding to Pasebaan. Who rides across Van B's path? Coachman of Van B has to stop dead. An affront—esp. as Van B has gold payong on carriage. Eng suggests to complain to HB II when they take leave. HB II criticizes conduct of Rongga. Rongga apologizes, saying action [was] not intentional. HB II asks Van B if R[angga]'s apology [is] enough.

---

#### Public Apology of Rongga
Van Braam says no—wants R[angga] to apologize in public before all *adi-dalems*. Which HB II immediately ordered and R[angga] carried out promptly. His foul mood with embarrassment in proper turns in a loud voice. Affair creates a sensation at court: amazement could be seen on everyone's face. Then toasts drunk and Van B, Eng take their leave. 

On return to Solo, another agreement reached at Klaten and [with] Patis regarding *gladhag*. Rongga also present at meeting—Van B treats him as though nothing had happened but face shows embarrassment. PWD also at meeting—that Yogya delegation in outwardly v. friendly fashion—appear to be greatest friends. 

Hendrik Deeken comes to Solo and would go on to Yogya. Sec[retary] Van B about to leave for Batavia and Van B asks D[eeken] to generate a letter to HB IV about his departure. 

---

## Project Ceremonial for Reception of Buyskes at Yogya 19 Nov. 1808

Project Ceremonial op de Receptie van den Heere Luitenant Gouverneur Generaal Buyskes aan het Hof van Z.H. den Sultan Hamengkoe Boewono Senopatti Ing Ngalogo Abdoel Rachman Sahidin Panoto Gomo Kalifatolah in kung Valek te Alg. Secretarie, 18 Ju. 1837. [^p142s-1]

1. Day Before arrival of Buyskes, Patih to go to Klaten. [^p142s-2]
2. Nayakas, Lt. Col. de Chasteauvieux to go as emissary of Res. Minister, + Cav. Capt. Mollet.
3. Princes of court to go to Prambanan one day before.
4. On arrival of Lt.-Gov., Dutch Flag to be flown from 4 bastions of fort.
5. At 7 o'clock Crown Prince + Nev. of Res. to go to Jenu.
6. At 6.30 Cav. to go to kraton to escort HB II to Demangan. (Compagnie Cav. to parade)
7. Receptions S.G.
8. At 6.00 aangaande/aangaande ministers to ride together in coach (Wiese + Engelhard) [^p142s-3] + 6 horses to reception at Demangan. HB II also to Demangan in another coach. On passing fort 21 gun salute + 3 salvoes from handgeweren [^p142s-4] of garrison. Salute repeated by field guns at Demangan immediately after HB II's arrival.
9. Bid Buyskes to be greeted at P + 21 gun salute + 3 salvoes from rifles by Sultan's Troops. Afterwards Buyskes/HB II to ride to kraton in same coach. Engelhard + CA followed by Wiese in separate coaches.
10. At Demangan, *pesanggrahan* set up where Buyskes can put on dress uniform. On passing fort 21 gun salute for Buyskes = garrison to be drawn up in mg. outside fort.
11. HB II's troops/pikemen to form a double rank along the way. But because returning MN Rycats [^p142s-5] would not stand, drawn up on road Yogya-Demangan [^p142s-6]. After visiting kraton, Buyskes to return to Engelhard at residency, musicians and paraded Grenadiers in honour. [^p142s-7]

---

13. Eng. to give buystes [briefings] in ceremonial fashion keys by Fort + Lt. Col. de Chasteauvieux to ask for any special mil. orders.

## CEREMONIAL FOR RECEPTIONS VAN DER CAPELLEN - YOGYA 1819

1. Asst. Res. Yogya to welcome [the Governor-General] at Kalijati nr. Kartasura. At Kalijati nr. Kartasura = Patih + 2 Nayakas.
2. Prince of court to greet G.G. at Klaten. Name as in 1817. [Marginalia: Pangéran 33 guns salute]
3. Pangéran putra at Kalasan = accompany G.G. to Bantulan.
4. Cav. to accompany Sultan to Bantulan = Dragoons.
5. HB IV + state coach Kyai Jimat to Demangan = Bantulan + co. Hussars / Royal prajurits.
6. Royal gamelan to play at Bantulan.
7. Paku Alam Res. to greet G.G. at Riksonegaran.

Art. 17: In Res. Van der Cap. + Mevrouw van der Cap. to take seats in Throne Room + Sultan on L. of Mrs. van der Cap.
[Marginalia: Res. [illegible] - Van der C. -> Mrs. van der C. - HB IV princes of blood. Rest + other officers on R. of van der Cap.]

Art. 24: Same seating arrangement adopted in pendhapa Srimenganti on visit of G.G. to kraton. Princes of blood to sit on floor on L. side HB IV. Rest officials to stand to R. of van der Cap.

Art. 25: Van der Caps. to be greeted by 33 shots from royal prajurits + gamelan to play at Mungang. [Marginalia: Lemadhanur]

Art. 26: With ceremonial HB IV to accompany van der Caps. back to Res. Srimenganti.

— Sept. 26. 1808 —
**Klaten Agreement**

Agreement
Yogya – Solo Sept. 26. 1808 (Klaten)
Sept. 26 1808 / 6 Ruwah Dal 1733:
Signed: J. A. van Braam, P. Engelhard. On order of Daendels.
Danuningrat (Solo) AR II (Yogya)

Murders at Tollgates / Borders Between Villages. Solo/Yogya.
Robberies Belonging to Princes / Government. Murders etc. [border areas]

1. All Bandhars to receive a letter + seal of the minister of Desa, to guard the tollgate. To provide men. To be watchful for property of the Princes. If tollgate robbed / burnt, inhabitants to answer for the damage.
2. If Desa attacked by robbers, others lying nearby to assist Desa attacked. Must follow robbers as long as tracks visible. To apprehend robbers + goods. Give notice Regents / AR II.
3. If Bekel cannot find robbers: To be stretched out in the sun on Pasirbaan [from] 7.00 am - 11.00 am for a week.
4. If [Bekel] allows robber to escape into his house — allowed 40 days [meanwhile] to apprehend them = Family taken from him.
5. If Mantri / Dampings suspect evil persons abiding in the Desa — not allowed to let them remain.
6. If travellers to stay in a village — to report first overnight to Pak Lurah.
7. If Regents do not obey — ornaments taken from them, suspended from offices.

***

- 8081 .DS .3032 -
Agreement / Felony Robbery

8. Subjects [Solo] accuse Yogya and vice-versa. But no proof = no case allowed. But accused must purge himself by taking an oath. But if accused by Solo/Yogya [affair to] be considered credible as a case.

9. If officials conspire & Rebels, they are to be punished & death or banished & wives/children to kraton.

10. Kamisepuhs eldest & Regents to be bound by an oath to act jointly & mantri Tampings.

11. Mantri Tampings must tell DRT if Kamisepuhs intrigue & robbers.

12. Guard on principle roads in area. Government lands to be provided by lurahs.

& similar agreement on gladhag.
16 Oct. 1808

Wiese to Daendels (Sec. Sev. Vecekens): Receives news about affair in Bantam: Official disclosure to be given to (explanation) Sultan of Yogya. (Letter from Sultan of Bantam to Sultan of Yogya). [Banishment of Sultan of Bantam].

**16 Dec 1808**
Wiese to Daendels (Sec. Sev. H. Vecekens): Question of theft of 48 buffaloes by Sunan's inhabitants and Blandongs (in Rembang area). A resolution to this must be forthcoming. Written explanation sent to Sunan.

#### Chronological Notes on Residency

*   **12 Aug 1808:** Decision of Daendels for Wiese to succeed as Resident of Yogya.
*   **4 Dec 1808:** Wiese installed as Resident. Report made in front of all officials and inhabitants of Yogya. [^p154s-1] Special ceremony in garden of Residency.
*   **5 Dec 1808:** J. W. Meens sent with a stamp/seal to Surabaya. [^p154s-2]
*   **5 Dec 1808:** Deserter found at Kutu—recognizable because of close-cropped hair. Taken to hospital in Yogya—but dies.
*   **9 Dec 1808:** Boats on Sala River to take salt from the Oosthoek. Go to Rajudan in Tuban area to take coke/paddy chaff. [^p154s-3] DR II sends a commission to Madiun to R. Rongga Prawiradirja to arrange for rice etc. to be dispatched down the Sala River. 
*   **As concerns Bandars on Sala River:** Boats should not pay any tax on rice or salt from Oosthoek.

---

**Wiese / Daendels**

**16 Dec. 1808**
Stealing of 48 buffaloes by Sultans Blandong people (earn their living from Rimbang [in forests]). Regent of Panolar, R.T. Natawijaya [transcribed as R.J.], asked to clear up the affair as soon as possible. Letter from HB II [^p155s-1] to Rongga, V. ca. Pasih, PWD II [^p155s-2]. Inhabitants to pay for buffaloes @ 5 Rood [^p155s-3] apiece/matter. 48 buffaloes from Blandongs (Tuban); 46 buffaloes from Trambalang.

Regent of Wirasari, R.T. Yudakusuma—Rongga to be told to see that inhabitants of Mancanagara lands do not mutilate government teak trees, often sorties into government forests.

**19 Dec. 1808**
Wiese / Daendels. Wachtmeester [^p155s-4] & Dragoons arrest 2 messengers of the Crown Prince coming from Demak to Semarang.

**4 Jan. 1809**
Wiese / Daendels.

**AN 'Yogya Brieven' no. 25: Events in Yogya 1809**

*   **1809 Jan 17-26:** Dagregister gehouden ter comptoire Djocjacarta Betreffende de het voorgevallene sedert primo Januari tot ultimo van Wintermaand 1809. Inspection journey to Yogya of Lt. Col. Comm. van Oorlog Baulard & Capt. Artillery Rauws & Lt. Nallat.
*   **27:** Garebeg Besar.
*   **15 Mar:** Lt. Col. de Chasteauvieux comm. of Yogya garrison leave to Semarang.

---

27 Apr.: Celebration of Garebeg Mulud.

5-8 May: Inspection journey to Boyolali by Minister Wiese, inspecting roads, bridges in Sultan's domains.

p. 53: Tax Return of 22 Apr. 1810 paid by Waterloo 60.000 + 21% Raipe to 12.600 [Note: Tax Return 1810]

17 May: Capt. Weerman of Artillery from Semarang to build new Arsenal in Yogya.

6 Jun.: Lt. Col. de Chasteauvieux leaves Yogya.

26 Jul.: DR II [Danureja II; transcribed as PR II] + 2 Inner Regents to Bandhar Pakis to greet Daendels, see Groc-hoff van Woortmann, 'Pagverhaal'.

2 Aug.: Return of Daendels to Semarang via Bojong, Kedu; DR II [Danureja II; transcribed as PR II] goes as far as Kemlakal; 2 Inner Regents to Semarang + Minister Wiese.

16 Aug.: 25 Ambonese soldiers arrive as strength of garrison.

2 Sept.: Celebration Birthday of King Louis of Holland. HB II + Crown Prince to Residency House; drink health of King Louis; 25 gun salute. Crown Prince + other courtiers stay to supper.

15 Sept.: Ex-Secretary G. van Woortmann leaves for Bat.

17 Sept.: Brig. de Kock + some reinforcements for Yogya garrison arrives.

18-19 Sept.: Visits HB II + return visit to Residency.

21 Sept.: De Kock + Wiese to N. coast at Kretik.

28 Sept.: [De Kock + Wiese] leaves for Semarang.

17 Nov.: Celebration of Garebeg Puasa.
Arsip Nasional No. 22.

Yogya 1808 (Dec) – 1809

Note that Sultan should give an instruction to the Regent of Panelang R.J. Narawijaya.

Letters dispatched by the Sultan (HB II) to Raden Rongga Prawiradirja (Madiun).

Total amount stolen:
* 48 buffaloes: Blandong (Tuban Res)
* 46 buffaloes: Blandong (Rrambalan)

Regent of Wirasari (R.T. Yudakusuma) must make good 7 buffaloes to Blandong of Mandutuku (in Rembang).

Rear that buffaloes would take a long time being returned because of attachment of Jan.

Ratu should give 5 Rmattan (Rondmatten) to chiefs of Blandong to buy good (Jipans buffaloes).

28 Jan Feast of (Bevar) inulnd to soon to receive an answer.]

Because ending of salutes at Sarebeg Bevar would cause much dishonour to Sultan & family - would continue old salutes until further word.

1809 (Yogya)

#### 1809
*   **[No. 22]**
    *   **Wiese / Dec. Gen. Veckert**
    *   **9 Jan 1809:** Arrival of Capt. Mollet & Dragoons to Yogya.
*   **Wiese / Daendels**
    *   **13 Jan 1809:** Mulno/April — Regent & families & followers from MN to Yogya. Need for building a new powder magazine.
*   **Wiese / Sec. Gen. Veckert**
    *   **14 Jan 1809:** 10 cases of opium needed for Yogya.
*   **Wiese / Daendels**
    *   **19 Jan 1809:** European coachman at Yogya, Major Johan Hedrik Bockhee deis. On advice of comm. Chasteauvieux = Corporal Lucas Lebeveld to be appointed sergeant [but too old for military service?].
*   **Wiese / Sec. Gen. Veckert**
    *   **22 Jan 1809:** 500 lbs of gunpowder allowed by Daendels for Jultan. After ceremony of Bedar at in Paderban, Crown Prince & various Yogya nobles to residency house; 50 lbs used for ceremonial valudes.
*   **Wiese / Daendels**
    *   **26 Jan 1809:** Rumours large mil. exercise (Solo). Rumour transmitted by Daendels that at Solo [a]nusually large military exercise (exacisies) takes place. R.J. Natadiningrat, Kyai Sindunagara [dispatched] to Solo to ascertain truth.
Provide troops for Daendels & plan to attack Yogya if auxiliary troops [are] forthcoming from Daendels.

Little disturbance caused in Yogya by this but worry about army manoeuvres by P. Prangwedana.

Col. Behrard & Capt. Raut pay compliments to Prangwedana [makes P.B.iv jealous; hence troop movements].

If English make an attack on coast of Java = Javanese reinforcement troops ready.

Letter Daendels to Sultan: Proceeding usual delivery of cartridges & bayonets. 500 lbs of gunpowder to be given each year.

1 Feb 1809 Wiese, Daendels: Danureja enjoins Wiese that Sultan HB II had given him an order to gather male inhabitants of town for military exercise. [Mil. Exercise]

Also orders sent out to all regents to hold themselves in readiness. [Clearly caused by recent events in Java] [No loss of disparagement to military exercises or P. Prangwedana/P.B.iv].

Impossible for Wiese to go to Sultan to remonstrate. Daendels fell from his carriage but leaves it to Dr. II.
A.N. No. 22.

1809 (Yogya)
1809 (Feb-April)

* Crown Prince visits Wiese & Sec. Woortman and Comm. de Chasteauvieux.
* Makes tour on horseback.

**2 Feb 1809**
Wiese -> Daendels: Letters MB II -> AR II cancelling army manoeuvres. (He should proceed to similar ridiculous action should be cleared from Java.)

**8 Feb 1809**
Wiese -> Van Braam (Ministre van): Collecting money for war treasury from citizen/EW. officials at Yogya:
* Lt Col de Chasteauvieux: 20,000 (Rendanten)
* W.H. Boev: 20,000
* Hospitaler Baumgarten: 15,000
* C.H. Klein: 25,000
* Capt Baksman: 10,000
* Citizen E. Kleung: 15,000

**11 Feb 1809**
Wiese -> Daendels: Desa Paderesan (in Yogya territory) plundered [by inhabitants] from Surakarta. Followed up by a detachment of mounted troops from Yogya.

**17 Mar 1809**
G.W. Wiese -> J.A. van Braam: Willem Baron van Reede tot de Parkeler leaves 39,000 sp. matten for P. Prangwedana.

**22 Mar 1809**
G.W. Wiese -> Van Braam: Each year Sunan/Wulwun allowed to load 6 Prauws tax free up Solo River to Gresik for purposes of trade. Fact that Sunan raises his number to 10 causes annoyance/jealousy of the Sultan, asks to be allowed 10 also.
**27 March 1809 (Weese to Van Braam)**
Refusal to put Bardhag Parawangan under Ondege Chinese, Jie Hockong. Message sent to R. Rongga (Madiun) and Sasrakusuma (Grobogan). R. Rongga expected in Yogya.

**2 April 1809 (Weese to Van Braam)**
People arrive from E. MN.

**11 April 1809 (Weese to Van Braam)**
R. Rongga arrives from Madiun for Puasa festival; at work face for Sultan.

**14 April 1809**
Questioning Piagem for Chinese tax-farmers, J. Gusti, in Grobogan area between Sasrakusuma and Chinese Jo Kong Ing.

**17 April 1809 (Weese to Daendels)**
Questioning teak wood trade which favors takes-out by the Mandog local Regents. Teak from E. MN in Blora. Chinese, other private persons to cut wood for Government.

---

**General Notes on Trade and Governance**
Regents, but particularly severe for the common man: because no rice cultivation and forced to make their living from collecting wood.

BW MB [1] wishes to end pinching wood in his teak forest: (for last 6 months).

Regents to be informed that teak forests would be hired from the Regents by the Government, but common men allowed to sell wood in small quantities to Government.

Important to keep the districts free of evil people—many come to that area from Pasir to cut down wood.

**26 April 1809**
Wiese & van Braam
*   PAYMENTS of JUSTICE by SUNAN/SULTAN in GRESIK ended.

**11 May 1809**
Wiese & van Braam
*   R. Rongga still loath to give over Bandhars & PANAWAN to Chinese: (without proper PASEN)

**2 June 1809**
Wiese & Daendels
*   PROJECT CEREMONIAL to receive Daendels at COURT in JULY 1809. Expecting much from Daendels.
*   Ceremonial for Reception of Daendels.

**13 June 1809**
Wiese & Daendels
*   MB [Mangkubumi] prepared to go to Semarang [Jenu] to meet Daendels (1 hour away) similar to protocol of visiting Sunan Buyukes 1808.
*   Asks for procession for CP [Crown Prince]:
    *   MB must sit higher than Daendels, below Res [Resident] or else Kraton.
    *   CP only very rarely sits on a stool (corner cog Dala).
*   MB to come as far as Jenu in Semarang area to meet Daendels & to be given a stool: also P. Ngabehi. Because had always been allowed one at official receptions in the Past.
*   Asks to be excused visit to Residency house, but would visit Daendels to the KRATON on following day.

27 June 1809
Wiese-Daendels: HB [Hare] pleased; allowed otools: Ngabeni

30 June 1809
Wiese-van Braam: Braam's letter complaining an attack / robbery in Brebes (Pekalongan) made by Yogya based men.
Orders to be given by Blora regent to hand over cutting teak wood to Dutch forestry officials.
Dr. H. sends letter to R. Rongga about this.

12 July 1809
Wiese-Daendels: 4 children [of] HB [Hare] get married. Necessary presents demanded.
Continual bothering of inhabitants of Pasisir by Yogya inhabitants; necessary measures to be taken in conjunction with HB.

24 July 1809
Wiese-Daendels: Commissioner assisted by a European in Japan [Jipang]. 5,000 daggers/knives (for wood cutting?) to be handed over by Regent there.
Daendels demand asks for validation.
[And] murder [of] European in that area.
Most serious talk to R. Rongga over it.

7 Aug 1809
Wiese-Daendels: Asks for bar iron for water fortress.

### (Yogya)

**4 Jan**
*   **From Batavia (?Wiese)**
    *   Treaty 9 July 1796 made with knowledge of M.B. II and Song Java [Van Overstraten] by Van IJsseldijk, D.R.I. concerning values to be given to Sultan / C.P. / Ratu / and other nobles on passing residency. Entitled to values: but not on extra occasions, only on evening. [^p173s-1]

**13 Jan**
*   **From Batavia**
    *   Necessary to build a new powder magazine: would be built at expense M.B. II but would have to await for monsoon (April 1809) when following old usage the M.N. Regents would arrive in Yogya.

**21 Jan**
*   **From Bat. to Wiese**
    *   Daendels complains about the attitude of R.I. concerning arrest of some "Jagers" (mounted volunteers). "You must tell this Patih, who has conducted himself improperly via M.B. II that 2nd time he has conducted himself so (Perendas G.B.I.) 3rd time a punishment would not fail to be given."

**In order to keep peace in Java:**
*   **Stationing of D.P. [^p173s-2]**
    *   Corps of Prangwedana – 3000 men to be stationed at Klaten.

**22 Jan 1809**
*   **From Bat.**
    *   Reestg Besar: C.P. and all nobles to come to Residency after ceremonies on Paser Besar – to drink healths and salutes for small (klein geschut).

**17 April**
*   **From Bat.**
    *   Demand (July 1808) of Daendels -> M.B. II to fell wood in Sultan's domains in Oosthoek for stakes at Fort Lodewijck (Surabaya). Demand completed at Menari stakes.

[Top margin notations: "1809", "1809"]

#### On arrival in Regents: Complaining Difficulties to HBII [^p174s-1]

**Complaint: E.MN Regents about Govnmt. Felling Timber?**
- E.MN Govnmt starts felling timber in their own woods/forest = affects their commerce.
- Given orders to their subordinates to fell wood but to no avail = because Govnmt takes it all for themselves.
- Can no longer farm out rights to fell timber to Chinese inhabiting N. coast.

Loose forestage means livelihoods = very hard for common man.
Few rice fields = produce little.
For great/small wood commerce means livelihood.

**14 April 1809**
Res Yogya -> Solo: Head Regent MN R. Rongga PWD III: arrives in Yogya. Discussion about timber — R.T. Sasrakusuma, Regent Grobogan.

**24 April 1809**
Pekalongan -> Yogya: Res P speaks about road through the wilderness of Kadawong.

**23 May 1809**
-> Bat: On felling timber: arrangement should be made for fair payment to common man & HBII's inhabitants as well as for the convenience of the Govnmt.

**10 June 1809**
-> Bat: Although dragoons must be absent for a short space of time from Yogya: HBII asks that dragoon should remain behind to guard the water gates and escort HBII when he appears on the Pasarbaan: according to old usage.

Surani/Juran informed that 39,500 Sp. Reals to be paid out to P. Prangwedana by Executors of Baron van Reede tot de Parkeler, because the sum was unlawfully retained by Reed from M.N.'s Estate.
(2nd & destroyed included in bundle)
Owing
Van Reede resided at Nt Res at Yala:
under pretext owing it under ordsog N.I. and as a guarantee of good behavior P. Prangwedana gives whole inheritance of M.N.
Keeps gold, precious stones and other precious to sum 39,500 sp. rs.
Refusal to return this sum: after a small payment to surviving inheritors.

P. Prangwedana claims it back
Phone N. Engelhard - Reported in N. ENG Geheim/Apart Missive 23 NOV 1803.
But claim dismissed 1 AUG 1805.
① Because Prangwedana = had only reclaimed money after death of Reede and until then been silent.
② That N. ENG who reports claim to N.I. Govmnt was the declared enemy of Van Reede.

∴ Request repeated by Prangwedana: 24 April 1808 - O.A. Van Braam:
and a Request to G.G. 15 May 1808

## Concerning Estate of Baron van Reede
#### Estate of P.W.P.
20 Mar 1809: R.T. Jastakuurima [or similar spelling] mentioned as Regent of Grobogan.

30 Nov 1809: District Jersaray, Desa Wanakrama, Yogyakarta: Ngabehi mentioned as Head of District.

30 Dec (Winter) Daendels:
Daendels writes about **Robberies in Pekalongan**:
- He demanded HB II to hand over guilty Heads of desas.
- Implied that HB II wants to put off decision until a further investigation.

- Personal visit of Daendels to Yogya (July 1809)
- Lessening of troops (Semarang)
- As result, repeated Sultan's lands would be fallen on by Daendels' troops within 14 days.
- At least Kidul would not be evacuated.
- Glory/greatness of HB II depends on attitude to Dutch government & behavior.

28 Dec 1809 (Wiese & HB II): Report on proposed attack on Kidul:
- Would occupy Kidul without exerting/handing it back.
- Messenger Citra Wiguna sent by Yogya Bupati R.T. Prawirawinata [to] Desa Tibosan (Demak) to investigate robbery.
- Because of unseemly conduct, captured and taken in detention to Semarang.
- Wiese reports: Daendels cites system: In Europe, heel wrong (well wrong). Does punished on the spot for deeds.

Daendels would await decision on Citra Wiguna:
- Because he demanded ORII to deliver up this person to Yogya.
- Worries: What HB II does about robberies in Pekalongan mounted by Yogya inhabitants of Wanakrama, Jersaray.

H 6986 | AvJ 1809 (Yogya) | -1809- | Ceremonial for visit of Daendels

10 June 1809 (from Bat.)

*   Discussion of Daendels' proposed visit to Yogya. Refusal of HBII to meet Daendels at Jenu, but when he met Lt. G. G. Buyskes in December 1808, only go as far as Demangan. 
*   According to Engelhard: Demangan - Onchom's distance from Yogya. HBII would go no further to meet Daendels.
*   Ask for a stool for CP at reception at Residency and at Kraton the following day because CP named as successor for a long time. 
*   HBII would not allow Wiese to stand in all ceremonies; HBII usually sits next to the Sultan.
*   ...asks Daendels for a stool for himself... CP/Ngabchi. 
*   HBII already wants to sit on throne in throne room in Residency House. 
*   In Kraton—Dampal ranked higher than Daendels, near by a bench.
*   HBII would take hand to see his beloved crown prince sitting on the floor. 
*   In Sala—CP always gets a stool from his father.

30 June 1809

*   Proces-Verbal de prefecture Tegal: Boundary of Sultan's land and district of Pamalang discussed.

9 August 1809 (from Bat.)

*   Note: Celebration/name day birthday of L. Napoleon usually occurs 2 September.

23 September 1809 (from Bat.)

*   Circular from Daendels to Lt. Capitan Cina: Not permitted to describe themselves as "Heads of Chinese Nation" because Chinese cannot possess her own nation.
11 April 1809 (to Bat.): HB II [^p178s-1] makes a visit (*een uitstapje*) to his son-in-law Natadiningrat. Goes in full state accompanied by armed dragoons. Arrival of Rongga PWD III [^p178s-2] & lesser native heads in Yogya.

2 Feb 1809 (from Bat.): Decision to abolish Prefectweg Rembang: To join: Regency Tuban / Prefectweg Gresek to Pref. Japara. Reg. Joana. Kabupaten Lasem / Rembang / Cengkal Sewu / Groggang / with direct admin. Colleges Forest.

11 Oct 1809 (from Bat.): Daendels gives orders to BURN HB II’s desas in Tegal border who had a hand in robbery Regency of Orebes (Kejawong). Daendels feels exceedingly provoked by constant marauding in *woodg* Kediri (Kadoe), which incurred much arson.

1 Aug 1809 (from Bat.): Payments strateged to Sala/Yogya – Sp.D. 10,000 = 12,500 Java money, Rood Realen.

14 Nov 1809 (from Bat.): After festival Puasa: HB II takes advantage of presence of many bupatis and workforces. 

[Marginal Note: *Pojok balowirti extended*] 

All pressed ganged into projecting the 4 points of the Kraton’s bastions 15-18'. DRII [^p178s-3] says that HB II does it to copy Dutch fortress; also we hear that HB II had put up the whole wall the Kraton in less than 3 months.

20 Nov (to Bat.): Death of Ratu Maduretna, wife of PWD III on 16 Nov.

#### Diary of Daendels’ visit to Yogya

**AN Yogya, Brieven 20 24.** 
**Signed Yogya, 17 Aug. 1809** 
**2 Aug. (28–31 July)**

**23 July 1809:** Daendels from Semarang to Salatiga. Received by Secretary of Sala. Daendels on horseback to Boyolali. Ampel met by Patih of Sala and 2 Wedanas. 11.30 arrival at Boyolali, met by Residents of Sala/Yogya ('Rimutas'), Patih Yogya, and P. Prangwedana. Returns to Yogya. Patih of Sala accompanies D[aendels] to Sala.

**24 July:** 6.30 D[aendels] from Boyolali to Banyudana. Met by troops of Prangwedana 'Legion', 28 guasaule. Assim—met by CP of Sala: to Kartasura. 2nd coach, Brig[adier] de Kock. Kartasura met by Ridder van Braam, Junant wedanas, Nayakas. Visit to Sala.

**28 July:** Visit to Yogya—proceed to Klaten. G.W. Wiese meets Daendels at Boyolali. Returns 3 days later. J. Groenhoff at Kartasura to meet Daendels, Asst-Resident. Kyai Jum. Sindunagara, P. Adinegara. Escort D[aendels] passed Pakis. Gezworen Translateur te Dimarang—C. Krijgsman. Klaten—Wiese, Mangkudiningrat, Mangkubumi greet Daendels.

**29 July:** Daendels leaves Klaten, arrives Prambanan 7.30. Visit to ruins / breakfast—dressed in full uniform of Napoleonic Marshal.

---

- Meets CP & Brothers at Kalassan.
- Met at 10.00 by Sultanat Bantulan: Cpt Wiese -> Reksanegara to receive Daendels.
- All MB II's prajurits = inter/outer Regents & all Dupatis ready at Reksanegara.
- 1.00 Wiese welcomes Daendels -> Yogya. Meeting in Throne Room MB II. Both sitting on throne: 28 gun salute. Daendels on right of MB II.
- Willem Dreesen presents keys of Fort via Wiese -> Daendels.
- Daendels invents Fort accompanied by MB II/CP.
- MB II met by Wiese in Kraton to make arrangements for the following day — "never in such a good humour," claps his hands, claps his knees.
- Pleased & 21 gun salute from the fort & small arms fire.
- 30 July: CP & 9. 2nd meeting of MB II & Daendels in Throne Room of Residency House.
- Daendels escorted to Kraton — passed the Fort. 28 gun salute for D from Fort.
- Passes broad row of Sultan's troops on alura.
- Meet MB II & D on Srimanganti — sit on throne: Refreshments.
- Visit to Ratus — children of MB II. Daendels embraced by [them], who has tears in his eyes. Happy to have the chance to meet D.
- 40 "Amazon" corps of women — ordered to appear by MB II.
- Show of mock tournaments.
- D accompanied back to Residency House by CP.
AN
'Yogya
Brieven'
no. 24

1809 2 Aug.
(28-30 July)
Daendels' visit to Yogya.

Fireworks in Residency garden (organized by Capitan Cina) in evening.

Supper in the Residency.

**31 July:**
Daendels rides out to HB II at Rajawinangun.
*   Pisanggrahan.
*   Wanacatur.
*   Parwareja.

Inspect buildings - fountains/Ngawarija. Well laid-out pools. Hunting places/underground passages/on top of mountains.
CP greets D at Rajawinangun.
D returns at 10 o'clock.

6.00 In evening taken by HB II to Kraton: meeting in Srimenganti.
D visits youngest and most cherished wife of HB II, Ratu Kencana Wulan.
Srimpi dances: 3 beautiful buildings placed at her disposal.
Supper for Daendels - Delicious Javanese dishes.
Friendly conversation HB II - Daendels.
9.30 - Daendels goes back, embraced by HB II at Srimenganti.

**1 August:**
CP to Residency house 8.00.
Accompanies D to Kraton.
Rampok Macan: watched for an hour with great pleasure.
After Rampok official leave taken of HB II.
Three Ratus present + children.
Talk 1/2 an hour.

**Afternoon: CP / HB¹ to Residency House:**

*   In Throne Room - Long talk of Daendels + HB over 'cultures' in Vh.
*   Long talk HB / CP.
*   Daendels in Residency house: agent duties of HB.
*   CP pays close attention.

**10.00:** Takes leave of Sultan after supper. Embraces Daendels v. warmly. Promises to remain in harmony + Dutch govt.

**2 August: 1809.**
**4.30 a.m.:** Daendels leaves Yogya.

*   **Delegate:** DR / Danukusuma. Ride out + HB → Kalaka.
*   Promise him a good journey.
*   → Bojong then Vecang.
*   .: Returns via Magelang → Semarang.

*   Wiese / R.T. Natadiningrat (Outer Bupati) + R.T. Sindunegara (Inner Bupati) accompany D → Vecang. 11.00 a.m. Remain the night.
*   Wiese takes leave of Daendels.
*   + Natadiningrat, Sindunegara. Return to Yogya.

## 1809 (Aug–Nov) – Yogya

**19 Aug 1809**
Wiese–Daendels
*   Birthday of King of Holland (Louis Napoleon) to be celebrated 2 Sept.
*   Wiese visits Pepper Estates in Loano.

> Pepper Estate Loano

**26 Aug 1809**
Wiese–Daendels
*   Pepper Estates very badly run.
*   European overseer not allowed to do anything useful, but Sultan of Adji Salen.
*   Not yet spoken to MBH about teak wood.
*   Japan wants the Javi wood, not burnt.

**29 Aug 1809**
Wiese–Daendels
*   Daendels letter to MBH handed over at formal ceremonial.

**3 Sept 1809**
Wiese–Daendels
*   MBH / CPH and children attend party in honor of Napoleon’s birthday in Res. House.
*   But were only there ½ hour (“because of great age”).

**12 Sept 1809**
Wiese–Daendels
*   Letter from Soldbach, Prefect, Grobogan Regent.
*   Varaknuma makes himself master of all rice/padi supplies destroyed for government.
*   At Bandhar Panawangan.
*   Details to question Bandhar:
    1. Rice in Grobogan v. badly stored this year.
    2. Regent pays farmer in kind, sells in money.
    3. Regent himself buys rice in outlying areas. Not anyone else allowed to buy rice too.

> Rice in Grobog an.

---

*   Regent buys up 140–150 *coyangs* of rice for about 25 R.D. Holland per *coyang*.

**29 Sept**
*Wiese-Daendels*
ROBBERS from CHERIBON BUT Jukes (Tumenggung Wijayakusuma) (Regent of Brebes) informs the DUNAN: NATIVE OPPRESSER—sent to Rembang, murdered in (Solo) BLORA Area when travelling through forest. Decision to investigate closely Regent of Blora & area in which murder takes place.

Kyai Haji MINHAD = joins him in Solo Desa & PABELLAN:
> Kyai Haji Minhad: Receives many Javanese, cruises to robbery & has opium—COULD BE DANGEROUS:

**12 Oct 1809**
*Wiese-Daendels*
CHINESE NO LONGER TO BE REGARDED AS A SEPARATE NATION [^p184s-1]

**17 Oct 1809**
*Wiese-Daendels*
Or that R.T. PRAWIRAWINATA goes to Demak area to enquire into argument between:
1. Al desa Jassasser
2. Bekel Nyuwa Citra Mesana
& 2 messengers from Yogya area.
Citrowijaya (Wakana) —> hath sent to present in Semarang

## CEREMONIAL for Reception of Daendels at Yogya:

1. Minister of Yogya to meet Daendels at Dayalali before D-day, Minister to return on Day D-a Yogya to Yogya.
2. 
    *   Veej Yogya to Kartasura.
    *   DRII / Comm.g Yogya to Pakis accompany Daendels to Klaten.
    *   At Klaten - Mangkudiningrat / Mangkubumi + Minister of Yogya.
3. Kalasan: CP + Sultan's children accompany him to Bantulan.
4. Dragoons to Keraton to accompany MBH to Bantulan.
6. MBH + all Kraton Prajurits to meet Daendels at Dimangan at 6 a.m. in coach Kyai Dimat.
8. Daendels to be greeted at Bantulan by gamelan at Bantulan Kraton.
9. CP + Minister to Reksanegaran to receive Daendels + ride w/ Daendels to Yogya escorted by Dragoons.

Meeting of Sultan with Daendels at Kastavna: [25 July 1809.]
Brigadier General De Kock in attendance [dag-verhaal] 23 July 1809.
Pekua Negara from Jono and Mangku Bumi at reception.

Daendels visits royal hunting lodges/gardens in Wonogny, Crown Prince [to Purwarejo, Kerta Winangoon, Wana Tjatoor.]

## 1809-10 D. Brieven no. 39.

- **Wiese to PR II, 1 Mar. 1809 (13 Sura 1736):** Asks PR II to tell MB II to stop troop manoeuvres, otherwise would jeopardise good relations before D's visit.
- **ID to ID, 5 Mar. 1809:** MB II should cancel order to MN Regents to hold themselves in readiness to march on Yogya.
- **Wiese to MB II, (n.d. Mar. 1809):** Embezzlement of Van Reede of 39,800 R.R. on death of MN I to be paid back to Prangwedana.
- **Wiese to PR II, 14 Mar. 1809:** News of new military manoeuvres, although no troops except usual detachments of MB II/CP allowed to be exercised.
- **ID to ID, 18 Mar. 1809:** MN Regents & work force expected from Regencies; Wiese asks PR II for 3rd time to see that necessary timber provided for building of fort.
- **Wiese to MB II, 4 Apr. 1809:** Telling of planned visit of D's parents to Yogya.
- **ID to PR II, 24 Apr. 1809:** Head bandar pays 60,000 [^p191s-1] for farm of jasan [^p191s-2] etc.
- **Wiese to MB II, 29 Apr. 1809:** Relating new troubles in Banten: Rebellion of Ng. Adamt, 5-6,000 followers v. Sultan.
- **[No Date/Author]:** Prej moorrees: wife, children attacked, moorrees severely wounded in carriage. Retiring to fort.
- **Wiese to PR II, 9 May 1809:** Roads/Bridges Yogya-Solo in miserable condition. Peelwijk difficult to get by on horseback.
- **ID to ID, 15 May 1809:** Groups of 40-60 soldiers touring vicinity of Chinese kampong by night.
- **ID to MB II, 31 May 1809:** Insp. Gen. of Forestry von Winckelmann to be allowed to enter into separate contracts with MN Regents for supply of Teak from MB II's lands.
- **ID to MB II, 31 May 1809:** D wants to inspect some of MB II's bodyguard in Residency during visit.
- **ID to MB II, 10 Jun. 1809:** If MB II refuses to accept ceremonial dictated by Daendels, D should not come to Yogya but limit his visit to Solo.
- **Wiese to MB II, 23 Jun. 1809:** Arrival of D at Semarang.
- **ID to PR II, 26 Jun. 1809:** Continuing lack of decisions MB II about project ceremonial for arrival of Daendels.
- **ID to ID, 1 Jul. 1809:** Road between Solo-Yogya still not in a good state.

---

- **29 Jul. 1809** (Weset to HB II): 500 logs. Being detained by bupatis in Jipang/Napan although had promised free transport to coast.

- **24 Sept. 1809** (Weset to PR II): Attacks on Tegal from Cirebon. 500-700 band of robbers roll on Regency of Brebes. Wood of Kaduan centre of robberies.

- **22 Oct. 1809** (ID to ID): Chinese make profit out of changing ronde realen into duit [platoons]. Employ Jav. women to [sit] in market to change money.

- **28 Oct. 1809** (ID to ID): 1st Lt Bonjart of Semarang Marine School to N. coast to survey area.

- **14 Jan. 1810** (ID to ID): Ratu Kenana Wulan trading in opium via middleman, Prayadiwangsa—Kotra Gidi.

- **[N.D.]** (Engelhard): Ridder van de Koninklijke Orde der Unie.

- **14 Sept. 1810** (Eng. to PR II): Work on fort at Klaten since 1808, v. little done on Yogya side—Surakarta work far more advanced. Despite terms of contract 1808 v. little advance on work. See log "Papers at Time of Capture."

- **14 Sept. 1810** (ID to ID): Time of renewal of lease of Pachtgeld falling due. Eng. asks Rijksnigara to ask HB II to allow tax to remain at 60,000 N.D. for period of 3 years.

- **23 Sept. 1810** (ID to HB II): If HB II does not provide, "Eclatante Satisfactie" for attack on Demak. Daendels would march a large army on Central Java. The least opposition on part of HB II would be taken as a declaration of war.

- **17 Sept. 1810** (ID to ID): Kuwong Demak and Nayeng Nikars to Kayumas. Finds only village inhabitants who flee along river. J. Sikan go to Hongue, find 9 deserters from Demak amongst them, 2 from Semarang Garrison Regt. PR II gives new orders to Yogya commissioners to Kayumas. R. Natakusuma / Ng. Wirjaja to arrest Demang of Kayumas and head of Wirasari and order R.T. Wirasari and regents of Grobogan to return to Yogya to answer for bad government.

[^p139s-1]: This document contains a handwritten archival summary, likely from a historical register or personal research notes, detailing colonial administrative and military activities between October and November 1808 in the Yogyakarta and Surakarta regions. Marginalia includes a circled note: "SEE OVER FOR WEALTHY IN A VILLAGE AT KLATEN", and a note in the lower right: "Sukab. 2 Mar 1838 no. 30 for Jaragan". Red ink annotations emphasize key military movements: "moves against Reds in Cirebon", "attack on Tegal from Payung Luhur", "price on head of Rangin", and "Ceremonial of Rear. Admiral Buyskes visit to Yogya 19 Nov. 1808".
